empty if none survived","maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","properties":{"file":{"description":"Repo-relative path of the file the finding is in","type":"string"},"line":{"description":"1-indexed line the finding anchors to","type":"integer","minimum":-9007199254740991,"maximum":9007199254740991},"summary":{"description":"One-sentence statement of the defect","type":"string"},"short_summary":{"description":"Compressed label for compact UI (≤60 chars): the claim alone, no rationale or consequence clause","type":"string","maxLength":60},"failure_scenario":{"description":"Concrete inputs/state → wrong output/crash","type":"string"},"category":{"description":"Short kebab-case slug of the finding type, e.g. \"correctness\", \"simplification\", \"efficiency\", \"test-coverage\"","type":"string","maxLength":40},"verdict":{"description":"Set when a verify pass ran; absent on inline-only reviews","type":"string","enum":["CONFIRMED","PLAUSIBLE"]},"outcome":{"description":"Set ONLY when re-reporting after applying fixes: what happened to this finding","type":"string","enum":["fixed","skipped","no_change_needed"]}},"required":["file","summary","failure_scenario"],"additionalProperties":false}}},"required":["findings"],"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"ScheduleWakeup","description":"Schedule when to resume work in /loop dynamic mode — the user invoked /loop without an interval, asking you to self-pace iterations of a specific task.\n\nDo NOT schedule a short-interval wakeup to poll for background work you started — when harness-tracked work finishes, you are re-invoked automatically, so polling is wasted. Instead schedule a long fallback (1200s+) so the loop survives if the work hangs or never notifies. The exception is external work the harness cannot track (a CI run, a deploy, a remote queue) — there, pick a delay matched to how fast that state actually changes.\n\nPass the same /loop prompt back via `prompt` each turn so the next firing repeats the task. For an autonomous /loop (no user prompt), pass the literal sentinel `<<autonomous-loop-dynamic>>` as `prompt` instead — the runtime resolves it back to the autonomous-loop instructions at fire time. (There is a similar `<<autonomous-loop>>` sentinel for CronCreate-based autonomous loops; do not confuse the two — ScheduleWakeup always uses the `-dynamic` variant.) To end the loop, call this tool with `stop: true` (omit every other field) — the loop ends immediately and no further wakeups fire.\n\nSet `noop: true` if nothing changed — you checked and there's nothing to report (\"no change\", \"still waiting\", \"quiet hold\"). Set `noop: false` if something happened worth keeping — you edited a file, posted a message, advanced state, or surfaced a finding. Consecutive `noop: true` ticks are collapsed in the user's terminal view and tracked as a streak, so long quiet holds stay legible to the user without scrolling. Omit `noop` when stopping (`stop: true`).\n\n## Picking delaySeconds\n\nThis session's requests use a 1-hour Anthropic prompt-cache TTL, so effectively every allowed delay (the runtime clamps to [60, 3600]) wakes up with your conversation context still cached. There is no cache cliff inside that range to pace around, and scheduling extra wakeups just to keep the cache warm is pure waste — never do that. (If the session enters usage overage, later requests drop to the 5-minute TTL; don't try to track or preempt that — the guidance here stays the same.)\n\nMatch the delay to what you're actually waiting for:\n\n- **Actively polling external state the harness can't notify you about** (a CI run, a deploy, a remote queue): pick the delay from how fast that state actually changes. A CI run that takes ~8 minutes deserves one ~480s check, not eight 60s ones.\n- **The long fallback heartbeat** (something else — a Monitor, a task notification — is the primary wake signal): 1200s+, so quiet wakeups stay rare.\n- **Idle ticks with no specific signal to watch**: default to **1200s–1800s** (20–30 min). The loop still checks back regularly, and the user can always interrupt if they need you sooner.\n\nDon't think in cache windows — think about what you're actually waiting for.\n\n## The reason field\n\nOne short sentence on what you chose and why. Goes to telemetry and is shown back to the user. \"watching CI run\" beats \"waiting.\" The user reads this to understand what you're doing without having to predict your cadence in advance — make it specific.\n","schema":{"name":"ScheduleWakeup","description":"Schedule when to resume work in /loop dynamic mode — the user invoked /loop without an interval, asking you to self-pace iterations of a specific task.\n\nDo NOT schedule a short-interval wakeup to poll for background work you started — when harness-tracked work finishes, you are re-invoked automatically, so polling is wasted. A CI run that takes ~8 minutes deserves one ~480s check, not eight 60s ones.\n- **The long fallback heartbeat** (something else — a Monitor, a task notification — is the primary wake signal): 1200s+, so quiet wakeups stay rare.\n- **Idle ticks with no specific signal to watch**: default to **1200s–1800s** (20–30 min). Required unless `stop` is true.","type":"boolean"}},"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"SendFeedback","description":"Use this tool to draft feedback about Claude Code when you hit a high-signal moment. That includes both PRODUCT issues and MODEL-BEHAVIOR issues:\n- a reproducible tool or product failure was just resolved or abandoned\n- the user clearly expressed frustration with Claude Code or with how you handled the task\n- you hit a missing capability that blocked a reasonable request\n- you notice, or the user points out, that your own behavior in this session went wrong, for example: you gave a confident answer then had to retract it; you stopped short and handed work back when you could have finished; you declined or disputed a reasonable request; you spawned more subagents than the task warranted; your tone was off; you asked more clarifying questions than needed; you expanded scope beyond what was asked\n\nThe draft is QUEUED LOCALLY. It is never sent without the user's explicit approval, and calling this tool renders no UI and does not interrupt the conversation, so never announce it or ask the user about it mid-task.\n\nWrite `details` as short labeled bullets in this exact order, one to three lines each, no narrative paragraphs:\n- **What happened:** the observed behavior vs. what was expected, with exact error text if short. Facts only.\n- **What the user said:** the user's own words that prompted this, quoted. If nothing did, write \"User didn't comment; observed by the model.\" Never paraphrase sentiment into a stronger claim.\n- **Repro:** the minimal steps or shape that reproduces it.\n- **Evidence:** identifiers a reader can chase, such as request IDs, timestamps, file paths, versions. Omit the bullet if there are none.\n\nConstraints:\n- Never fabricate or exaggerate user sentiment; report only what actually happened.\n- Everything in the draft must be sourced from the user or the session, never inferred: leave unknown fields blank rather than guess, and add a final **Cause:** bullet only for a root cause you verified in-session.\n- Use `area` to name the part of Claude Code the feedback is about (a feature, command, or workflow, e.g. \"hooks config\", \"/help\", \"file editing\") when there is a clear one; leave it blank otherwise.\n- Use `failure_mode` ONLY when the report is about model behavior (how Claude responded), not a product bug. Pick the single closest value, or `other` when it is a model-behavior issue that fits no listed value; omit the field only when the report is a product/tool bug with no model-behavior component.\n- Use `task_category` to name what kind of task the session was doing, or `other` when it is a clear task that fits no listed value. Omit only if genuinely unclear.\n- Do not include secrets or credentials. Refer to people by role (\"a teammate\", \"the PR reviewer\"), never by name, email address, or chat/user ID. This applies inside quoted user words too: replace a name or handle with a bracketed role (e.g. \"[a teammate]\") and keep the rest verbatim. Do not include customer-facing channel or DM IDs, or excerpts of customer content. Session, request, and run IDs, timestamps, repo/PR numbers, and file paths (written relative to the working directory, or ~-prefixed, not absolute paths under the user's home) remain the right evidence.\n- If the issue looks like a security vulnerability: describe the class of problem, never a working exploit or step-by-step extraction path.\n- Draft only at the natural moments listed above, and at most one draft per distinct issue; never re-draft the same issue in a session.","schema":{"name":"SendFeedback","description":"Use this tool to draft feedback about Claude Code when you hit a high-signal moment. Omit the bullet if there are none.\n\nConstraints:\n- Never fabricate or exaggerate user sentiment; report only what actually happened.\n- Everything in the draft must be sourced from the user or the session, never inferred: leave unknown fields blank rather than guess, and add a final **Cause:** bullet only for a root cause you verified in-session.\n- Use `area` to name the part of Claude Code the feedback is about (a feature, command, or workflow, e.g. \"hooks config\", \"/help\", \"file editing\") when there is a clear one; leave it blank otherwise.\n- Use `failure_mode` ONLY when the report is about model behavior (how Claude responded), not a product bug. Pick the single closest value, or `other` when it is a model-behavior issue that fits no listed value; omit the field only when the report is a product/tool bug with no model-behavior component.\n- Use `task_category` to name what kind of task the session was doing, or `other` when it is a clear task that fits no listed value. {"parentUuid":"1baa3ac9-edc2-4708-8ff3-8838ff210a2f","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fc58ea97-0f04-4590-9bfb-61bf232fbcef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WT8uLL6jYDLUV1n2pD5YGY","type":"tool_result","content":"6 app/__init__.py\n32 app/authz.py\n124 app/cli.py\n59 app/content.py\n44 app/deploy.py\n77 app/main.py\n26 app/markdown.py\n151 app/pages.py\n140 app/posts.py\n190 app/publish.py\n37 app/ratelimit.py\n96 app/security.py\n33 app/settings.py\n9 app/templating.py\n201 app/users.py\n0 app/routes/__init__.py\n46 app/routes/auth.py\n48 app/routes/dashboard.py\n175 app/routes/pages.py\n140 app/routes/posts.py\n44 app/routes/user_password.py\n140 app/routes/users.py\n9 templates/admin/_items.html\n14 templates/admin/change_password.html\n30 templates/admin/content.html\n43 templates/admin/dashboard.html\n8 templates/admin/forbidden.html\n11 templates/admin/login.html\n48 templates/admin/page_form.html\n15 templates/admin/pages.html\n39 templates/admin/post_form.html\n15 templates/admin/posts.html\n9 templates/admin/preview.html\n27 templates/admin/user_edit.html\n17 templates/admin/user_new.html\n8 templates/admin/user_temp_password.html\n23 templates/admin/users.html\n22 templates/public/home.html\n9 templates/public/news.html\n8 templates/public/page.html\n9 templates/public/post.html\n118 tests/conftest.py\n277 tests/test_posts.py\n290 tests/test_publish.py\n79 scripts/seed_demo.py\nΣ 2946\n\"\"\"Post storage. A Post's Byline is copied from its author at first Published.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    slug text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references users(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    published_at text,\n    slug_locked integer not null default 0,\n    byline_name text,\n    byline_title text,\n    byline_term text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", title).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", text.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef init_posts(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n                    \" on posts.author_id = users.id where posts.id=?\",\n                    (post_id,)).fetchone()\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            elif action == \"draft\":\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set status='draft' where id=?\",\n                             (post_id,))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n    if first:  # published timestamp, Byline and Slug lock are fixed at first publish\n        conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set published_at=?, slug_locked=1, byline_name=?,\"\n            \" byline_title=?, byline_term=? where id=?\",\n            (stamp, author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"], author[\"term\"],\n             post_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update posts set slug_locked=0 where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef format_byline(name, title, term) -> str:\n    return \", \".join(p for p in (name, title, term) if p)\n\n\ndef byline(post) -> str:\n    return format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                         post[\"byline_term\"])\n\"\"\"One view over Posts and Pages for the dashboard and the content list.\n\nAn item's \"owner\" is its author (Post) or its Assigned editor (Page), so\n\"Mine\" and the author filter treat both kinds the same way.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.deploy import last_deploy\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_ITEMS = \"\"\"\nselect 'post' as kind, posts.id, posts.title, posts.status, posts.updated_at,\n       posts.author_id as owner_id, users.display_name as owner_name\n  from posts join users on users.id = posts.author_id\nunion all\nselect 'page', pages.id, pages.title, pages.status, pages.updated_at,\n       pages.assigned_editor_id, users.display_name\n  from pages left join users on users.id = pages.assigned_editor_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef list_items(database: Path, *, status: str = \"\", kind: str = \"\",\n               owner_id: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Posts and Pages, newest change first, each marked with `changed`\n    (changed since the last Deploy) and `pending` (needs releasing).\"\"\"\n    clauses, params = [], []\n    if status in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        clauses.append(\"status = ?\")\n        params.append(status)\n    if kind in (\"post\", \"page\"):\n        clauses.append(\"kind = ?\")\n        params.append(kind)\n    if owner_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"owner_id = ?\")\n        params.append(owner_id)\n    where = \" where \" + \" and \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(f\"select * from ({_ITEMS}){where}\"\n                            \" order by updated_at desc, kind, id desc\",\n                            params).fetchall()\n    deployed_at = last_deploy(database)\n    items = []\n    for row in rows:\n        item = dict(row)\n        item[\"changed\"] = (item[\"status\"] == \"published\" if deployed_at is None\n                           else item[\"updated_at\"] >= deployed_at)\n        item[\"pending\"] = item[\"status\"] == \"published\" and item[\"changed\"]\n        items.append(item)\n    return items\n\n\ndef unassigned_pages(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select id, title from pages\"\n                            \" where assigned_editor_id is null\"\n                            \" order by title\").fetchall()","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f4a4583a-5e72-48bd-a63a-26788d221722","timestamp":"2026-10-21T00:29:01.955Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"6 app/__init__.py\n32 app/authz.py\n124 pages.id, pages.title, pages.status, pages.updated_at,\n       pages.assigned_editor_id, users.display_name\n  from pages left join users on users.id = pages.assigned_editor_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef list_items(database: Path, *, status: str = \"\", kind: str = \"\",\n               owner_id: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Posts and Pages, newest change first, each marked with `changed`\n    (changed since the last Deploy) and `pending` (needs releasing).\"\"\"\n    clauses, params = [], []\n    if status in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        clauses.append(\"status = ?\")\n        params.append(status)\n    if kind in (\"post\", \"page\"):\n        clauses.append(\"kind = ?\")\n        params.append(kind)\n    if owner_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"owner_id = ?\")\n        params.append(owner_id)\n    where = \" where \" + \" and \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(f\"select * from ({_ITEMS}){where}\"\n                            \" order by updated_at desc, kind, id desc\",\n                            params).fetchall()\n    deployed_at = last_deploy(database)\n    items = []\n    for row in rows:\n        item = dict(row)\n        item[\"changed\"] = (item[\"status\"] == \"published\" if deployed_at is None\n                           else item[\"updated_at\"] >= deployed_at)\n        item[\"pending\"] = item[\"status\"] == \"published\" and item[\"changed\"]\n        items.append(item)\n    return items\n\n\ndef unassigned_pages(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select id, title from pages\"\n                            \" where assigned_editor_id is null\"\n                            \" order by title\").fetchall()","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"classifierBoundary":true,"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"89c2fdd6-4f67-45ad-8ec5-728872519b3e","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":null,"status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":2},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1baa3ac9-edc2-4708-8ff3-8838ff210a2f","permissionDecision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reasonType":"classifier"},"session_id":"ced06a84-a7f0-44aa-b053-f8a4a8bc0e50","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"ced06a84-a7f0-44aa-b053-f8a4a8bc0e50","version":"2.1.305","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"7d9f7cd9-f606-4c83-b07c-1ce4b8d67897","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fc58ea97-0f04-4590-9bfb-61bf232fbcef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0195qjH3vneShG3reWFEoPTA","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Posts: list, create, edit, publish, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import authz, posts, users\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.publish import navigation\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\nguarded = [Depends(current_user), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nACTIONS = {\"save\", \"publish\", \"draft\"}\n\n\ndef load_post(request: Request, user, post_id: int, allowed) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fetch a Post the current User is allowed to act on, or 404/403.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    if not allowed(user, post):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"You can only change your own Posts.\")\n    return post\n\n\ndef render_form(request: Request, user, *, post=None, form=None, error=None,\n                status_code=200):\n    if form is None:\n        form = ({\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": post[\"body\"]}\n                if post else {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post else \"New post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form\": form, \"error\": error, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"byline\": posts.byline(post) if post and post[\"published_at\"] else None,\n         \"can_edit_slug\": authz.can_edit_slug(user, post) if post else True,\n         \"can_unlock_slug\": bool(post and post[\"slug_locked\"]\n                                 and authz.can_unlock_slug(user)),\n         \"can_delete\": bool(post and authz.can_delete_post(user, post))},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/posts.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"posts\": posts.list_posts(request.app.state.database),\n         \"can_edit\": HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. annotations\n\nimport re\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom markupsafe import escape\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import format_byline\nfrom app.users import connect\n\nCSS = (settings.STATIC / \"style.css\").read_text()\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\nclass DraftInOutput(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A Draft's title or Slug would appear in the generated site.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _rows_where(database: Path, table: str, where: str, order: str) -> list:\n    if not database.exists():\n        return []\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if not conn.execute(\"select 1 from sqlite_master where name=?\",\n                            (table,)).fetchone():\n            return []\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"select * from {table} where {where} order by {order}\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef _posts_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    return _rows_where(database, \"posts\", where, \"published_at desc, id desc\")\n\n\ndef _pages_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    return _rows_where(database, \"pages\", where, \"nav_order, title, id\")\n\n\ndef _published_pages(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _pages_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_pages(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _pages_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _published_posts(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_rows(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _first_paragraph(html: str) -> str:\n    match = re.search(r\"<p>.*?</p>\", html, re.S)\n    return match.group(0) if match else \"\"\n\n\ndef _check_no_drafts(site: Path, database: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Refuse if any Draft made it into any page of the site.\n\n    Every post entry carries a data-post-id marker (pages a data-page-id one) and\n    links to posts/<slug>.html (pages/<slug>.html). Both are compared with the\n    Drafts straight from the database (ids and Slugs are unique within a kind),\n    on every file, so a Draft leaking onto Home, News or the Navigation is\n    caught as surely as one on its own page. Titles are not matched: a Draft\n    called \"News\" must not block every publish.\n    \"\"\"\n    kinds = ((\"post\", \"posts\", _draft_rows(database)),\n             (\"page\", \"pages\", _draft_pages(database)))\n    for f in sorted(site.rglob(\"*\")):\n        if not f.is_file():\n            continue\n        text = f.read_text()\n        for marker, folder, drafts in kinds:\n            marked = {int(i) for i in re.findall(rf'data-{marker}-id=\"(\\d+)\"', text)}\n            for draft in drafts:\n                if (draft[\"id\"] in marked or f\"{folder}/{draft['slug']}.html\" in text\n                        or f.name == f\"{draft['slug']}.html\"\n                        and f.parent.name == folder):\n                    raise DraftInOutput(\n                        f\"Refusing to publish: the Draft \\\"{draft['title']}\\\" would \"\n                        f\"appear in {f.relative_to(site)}. Drafts must never leave \"\n                        \"the database. The site was not written.\")\n\n\ndef render(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Render Published content into `out`; return it and a list of rendered items.\n\n    Builds in a sibling folder and swaps it in only after the Draft check\n    passes, so a refusal never leaves a half-finished site.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    work = out.parent / (out.name + \".building\")\n    if work.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(work)\n    work.mkdir(parents=True)\n    try:\n        items = _render_into(work, database)\n    except BaseException:\n        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)\n        raise\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    work.rename(out)\n    return out, items\n\n\ndef navigation(database: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Navigation as (label, site-relative href): Home, then the Published Pages\n    marked for it in order, then News. The Publish run and Preview share this.\"\"\"\n    return ([(\"Home\", \"index.html\")]\n            + [(p[\"title\"], f\"pages/{p['slug']}.html\")\n               for p in _published_pages(database)\n               if p[\"show_in_nav\"] and not p[\"is_home\"]]\n            + [(\"News\", \"news.html\")])\n\n\ndef _render_into(work: Path, database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    env = environment()\n    published = _published_posts(database)\n    entries = []\n    for post in published:\n        body = render_markdown(post[\"body\"])\n        entries.append({\n            \"id\": post[\"id\"], \"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": body,\n            \"excerpt\": _first_paragraph(body),\n            \"date\": (post[\"published_at\"] or \"\")[:10],\n            \"byline\": format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                                    post[\"byline_term\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"posts/{post['slug']}.html\",\n        })\n\n    page_entries = []\n    for page in _published_pages(database):\n        page_entries.append({\n            \"id\": page[\"id\"], \"title\": page[\"title\"], \"body\": render_markdown(page[\"body\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"pages/{page['slug']}.html\", \"in_nav\": bool(page[\"show_in_nav\"]),\n            \"is_home\": bool(page[\"is_home\"])})\n    home_page = next((p for p in page_entries if p[\"is_home\"]), None)\n\n    (work / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (work / \"posts\").mkdir()\n    (work / \"pages\").mkdir()\n\n    links = navigation(database)\n\n    def write(path: str, template: str, prefix: str, **ctx) -> None:\n        # prefix is \"\" for top-level pages and \"../\" for pages in posts/ and pages/\n        (work / path).write_text(env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, subtitle=settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n            css_path=prefix + \"style.css\",\n            home_path=prefix + \"index.html\", news_path=prefix + \"news.html\",\n            nav_links=[(label, prefix + href) for label, href in links],\n            **ctx))\n\n    write(\"index.html\", \"public/home.html\", \"\", items=entries[:5], home_page=home_page)\n    write(\"news.html\", \"public/news.html\", \"\", items=entries)\n    for e in entries:\n        write(e[\"href\"], \"public/post.html\", \"../\", post=e)\n    for p in page_entries:\n        write(p[\"href\"], \"public/page.html\", \"../\", page=p)\n    _check_no_drafts(work, database)\n    return ([\"Home (index.html)\", \"News (news.html)\"]\n            + [f\"Page: {p['title']} ({p['href']})\" for p in page_entries]\n            + [f\"Post: {e['title']} ({e['href']})\" for e in     \"nav_preview\": True})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n           slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: error=\"A Post needs a title.\",\n                           status_code=400)\n    try:\n        post_id = posts.create_post(\n            request.app.state.database, author=user, title=title.strip(),\n            slug=posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else \"\", body=body,\n            publish=action == \"publish\")\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id:int}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n         action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n    if action not in ACTIONS:\n        raise HTTPException(400, \"Unknown action.\")\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    new_slug = None\n    if authz.can_edit_slug(user, post):\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else post[\"slug\"]\n    try:\n        posts.update_post(request.app.state.database, post_id, title=title.strip(),\n                          body=body, slug=new_slug, action=action)\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/unlock-slug\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef unlock_slug(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    if not authz.can_unlock_slug(user):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Only the Admin can unlock a Slug.\")\n    if posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id) is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    posts.unlock_slug(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/delete\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef delete(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)\n    posts.delete_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom markupsafe import escape\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import format_byline\nfrom app.users import connect\n\nCSS = (settings.STATIC / \"style.css\").read_text()\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\nclass DraftInOutput(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A Draft's title or Slug would appear in the generated site.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _rows_where(database: Path, table: str, where: str, order: str) -> list:\n    if not database.exists():\n        return []\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        if not conn.execute(\"select 1 from sqlite_master where name=?\",\n                            (table,)).fetchone():\n            return []\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"select * from {table} where {where} order by {order}\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef _posts_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    return _rows_where(database, \"posts\", where, \"published_at desc, id desc\")\n\n\ndef _pages_where(database: Path, where: str) -> list:\n    return _rows_where(database, \"pages\", where, \"nav_order, title, id\")\n\n\ndef _published_pages(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _pages_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_pages(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _pages_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _published_posts(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status='published'\")\n\n\ndef _draft_rows(database: Path) -> list:\n    return _posts_where(database, \"status!='published'\")\n\n\ndef _first_paragraph(html: str) -> str:\n    match = re.search(r\"<p>.*?</p>\", html, re.S)\n    return match.group(0) if match else \"\"\n\n\ndef _check_no_drafts(site: Path, database: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Refuse if any Draft made it into any page of the site.\n\n    Every post entry carries a data-post-id marker (pages a data-page-id one) and\n    links to posts/<slug>.html (pages/<slug>.html). Both are compared with the\n    Drafts straight from the database (ids and Slugs are unique within a kind),\n    on every file, so a Draft leaking onto Home, News or the Navigation is\n    caught as surely as one on its own page. Titles are not matched: a Draft\n    called \"News\" must not block every publish.\n    \"\"\"\n    kinds = ((\"post\", \"posts\", _draft_rows(database)),\n             (\"page\", \"pages\", _draft_pages(database)))\n    for f in sorted(site.rglob(\"*\")):\n        if not f.is_file():\n            continue\n        text = f.read_text()\n        for marker, folder, drafts in kinds:\n            marked = {int(i) for i in re.findall(rf'data-{marker}-id=\"(\\d+)\"', text)}\n            for draft in drafts:\n                if (draft[\"id\"] in marked or f\"{folder}/{draft['slug']}.html\" in text\n                        or f.name == f\"{draft['slug']}.html\"\n                        and f.parent.name == folder):\n                    raise DraftInOutput(\n                        f\"Refusing to publish: the Draft \\\"{draft['title']}\\\" would \"\n                        f\"appear in {f.relative_to(site)}. Drafts must never leave \"\n                        \"the database. The site was not written.\")\n\n\ndef render(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Render Published content into `out`; return it and a list of rendered items.\n\n    Builds in a sibling folder and swaps it in only after the Draft check\n    passes, so a refusal never leaves a half-finished site.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    work = out.parent / (out.name + \".building\")\n    if work.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(work)\n    work.mkdir(parents=True)\n    try:\n        items = _render_into(work, database)\n    except BaseException:\n        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)\n        raise\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    work.rename(out)\n    return out, items\n\n\ndef navigation(database: Path) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Navigation as (label, site-relative href): Home, then the Published Pages\n    marked for it in order, then News. The Publish run and Preview share this.\"\"\"\n    return ([(\"Home\", \"index.html\")]\n            + [(p[\"title\"], f\"pages/{p['slug']}.html\")\n               for p in _published_pages(database)\n               if p[\"show_in_nav\"] and not p[\"is_home\"]]\n            + [(\"News\", \"news.html\")])\n\n\ndef _render_into(work: Path, database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    env = environment()\n    published = _published_posts(database)\n    entries = []\n    for post in published:\n        body = render_markdown(post[\"body\"])\n        entries.append({\n            \"id\": post[\"id\"], \"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": body,\n            \"excerpt\": _first_paragraph(body),\n            \"date\": (post[\"published_at\"] or \"\")[:10],\n            \"byline\": format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                                    post[\"byline_term\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"posts/{post['slug']}.html\",\n        })\n\n    page_entries = []\n    for page in _published_pages(database):\n        page_entries.append({\n            \"id\": page[\"id\"], \"title\": page[\"title\"], \"body\": render_markdown(page[\"body\"]),\n            \"href\": f\"pages/{page['slug']}.html\", \"in_nav\": bool(page[\"show_in_nav\"]),\n            \"is_home\": bool(page[\"is_home\"])})\n    home_page = next((p for p in page_entries if p[\"is_home\"]), None)\n\n    (work / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (work / \"posts\").mkdir()\n    (work / \"pages\").mkdir()\n\n    links = navigation(database)\n\n    def write(path: str, template: str, prefix: str, **ctx) -> None:\n        # prefix is \"\" for top-level pages and \"../\" for pages in posts/ and pages/\n        (work / path).write_text(env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, subtitle=settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n            css_path=prefix + \"style.css\",\n            home_path=prefix + \"index.html\", news_path=prefix + \"news.html\",\n            nav_links=[(label, prefix + href) for label, href in links],\n            **ctx))\n\n    write(\"index.html\", \"public/home.html\", \"\", items=entries[:5], home_page=home_page)\n    write(\"news.html\", \"public/news.html\", \"\", items=entries)\n    for e in entries:\n        write(e[\"href\"], \"public/post.html\", \"../\", post=e)\n    for p in page_entries:\n        write(p[\"href\"], \"public/page.html\", \"../\", page=p)\n    _check_no_drafts(work, database)\n    return ([\"Home (index.html)\", \"News (news.html)\"]\n            + [f\"Page: {p['title']} ({p['href']})\" for p in page_entries]\n            + [f\"Post: {e['title']} ({e['href']})\" for e in entries])\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None, database: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    return render(out, database)[0]\n\"\"\"The dashboard and the content list.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__       \"pending\": [i for i in items if i[\"pending\"]],\n         \"drafts\": [i for i in items if i[\"status\"] == \"draft\"],\n         \"recent\": [i for i in items if i[\"owner_id\"] == \"mine\": mine}})\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"{{ url_for('list_view') }}\">All posts</a></p>\n  {% if post %}\n    <p>Status: <strong  <a href=\"{{ url_for('content_list') }}\">Clear</a>\n  </form>\n  {% if not items %}<p>Nothing matches.</p>{% endif %}\n  <ul class=\"items\">{% for i in items %}{{ row(i, user, can_edit) }}{% endfor {"parentUuid":"a90411b6-e18f-42c9-9c24-970736c49efa","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fc58ea97-0f04-4590-9bfb-61bf232fbcef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RwqRa9x6iNVtzgA4a64HPU","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\nEvery app built here runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the\ndemo users below.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.ratelimit import login_limiter\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\",\n              \"display_name\": \"Demo Admin\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\",\n               \"display_name\": \"Demo Editor\"},\n}\n\n\ndef token_from(html: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token field\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    users.init_db(path)\n    for role, info in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        users.create_user(path, email=info[\"email\"], password=info[\"password\"],\n                          role=role, display_name=info[\"display_name\"])\n    return path\n\n\nclass FrozenClock:\n    \"\"\"A clock tests move by hand; pass it to create_app(clock=...).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef reset_login_limiter():\n    \"\"\"The limiter is process-wide; start and finish every test with it empty.\"\"\"\n    login_limiter.reset()\n    yield\n    login_limiter.reset()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock():\n    return FrozenClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef locked_client(db_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"An anonymous client whose login limiter runs on the frozen `clock`.\"\"\"\n    return TestClient(create_app(database=db_path, clock=clock))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_client(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Build a fresh, anonymous client against the temporary database.\"\"\"\n\n    def _make() -> TestClient:\n        return TestClient(create_app(database=db_path))\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_client) -> TestClient:\n    return make_client()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_client):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = make_client()\n        form = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if form.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token_from(form.text)},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        # A failed login re-renders the form with a 200, so only a redirect\n        # proves the session was started.\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}: expected a redirect\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import pages, posts, settings, users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    for email, name, role, password in (\n        (\"admin@example.test\", \"Demo Admin\", \"admin\", admin_pw),\n        (\"editor@example.test\", \"Demo Editor\", \"editor\", editor_pw),\n    ):\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=email,\n                              password=password, role=role, display_name=name)\n            print(f\"Created {role} {email}\")\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{email} already exists; left as is\")\n    posts.init_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    editor = users.authenticate(settings.DATABASE_PATH, \"editor@example.test\",\n                                editor_pw)\n    existing = {p[\"slug\"] for p in posts.list_posts(settings.DATABASE_PATH)}\n    for title, body, publish in (\n        (\"Welcome to the new chapter site\",\n         \"The **Chi Chapter** now keeps its own news. Read more on our \"\n         \"[chapter page](https://example.org).\", True),\n        (\"Rush announcement (draft)\", \"Dates still to be confirmed.\", False),\n    ):\n        if posts.slugify(title) in existing:\n            continue\n        posts.create_post(settings.DATABASE_PATH, author=editor, title=title,\n                          slug=\"\", body=body, publish=publish)\n        print(f\"Created {'Published' if publish else 'Draft'} post: {title}\")\n    pages.init_pages(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    existing_pages = {p[\"slug\"] for p in pages.list_pages(settings.DATABASE_PATH)}\n    for title, body, publish, nav, order, home, assign in (\n        (\"Welcome\", \"We are the **Chi Chapter** of Delta Tau Delta at Kenyon College.\",\n         True, False, 0, True, False),\n        (\"About\", \"Who we are and what we do.\", True, True, 1, False, False),\n        (\"Community Service\", \"Our service work. Dates still to be confirmed.\",\n         False, True, 2, False, True),\n    ):\n        if pages.slugify(title) in existing_pages:\n            continue\n        pages.create_page(settings.DATABASE_PATH, title=title, slug=\"\", body=body,\n                          assigned_editor_id=editor[\"id\"] if assign else None,\n                          show_in_nav=nav, nav_order=order, is_home=home,\n                          publish=publish)\n        print(f\"Created {'Published' if publish else 'Draft'} page: {title}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\n\n\ndef _load_dotenv(path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Copy KEY=value lines from .env into the environment (never overriding).\"\"\"\n    if not path.exists():\n        return\n    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():\n        line = line.strip()\n        if line and not line.startswith(\"#\") and \"=\" in line:\n            key, _, value = line.partition(\"=\")\n            os.environ.setdefault(key.strip(), value.strip().strip(\"\\\"'\"))\n\n\n_load_dotenv(ROOT / \".env\")\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSTATIC = ROOT / \"app\" / \"static\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nDEV_SECRET_KEY = \"dev-only-not-for-production\"\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", DEV_SECRET_KEY)\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"Delta Tau Delta at Kenyon College\")\nSITE_SUBTITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_SUBTITLE\", \"Chi Chapter\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n\"\"\"T03: `cms publish`, tested at render_site() and the CLI.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import posts, users\nfrom app.cli import main\nfrom app.publish import DraftInOutput, render_site\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef author(db_path):\n    posts.init_posts(db_path)\n    return users.get_user(db_path, 2)  # the seeded editor\n\n\ndef add(db_path, author, title, body=\"Body text.\", slug=\"\", publish=True):\n    return posts.create_post(db_path, author=author, title=title, slug=slug,\n                             body=body, publish=publish)\n\n\ndef site_text(out):\n    return {p: p.read_text() for p in out.rglob(\"*\") if p.is_file()}\n\n\ndef test_home_news_and_post_pages(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, author, \"Rush Night\", body=\"First para.\\n\\nSecond para.\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    home = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in home and \"First para.\" in home\n    assert \"Second para.\" not in home\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in (out / \"news.html\").read_text()\n    page = (out / \"posts\" / \"rush-night.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page and \"Second para.\" in page\n\n\ndef test_home_shows_only_5_latest_news_lists_all_newest_first(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    for i in range(7):\n        pid = add(db_path, author, f\"Story {i}\")\n        with posts.connect(db_path) as conn:\n            conn.execute(\"update posts set published_at=? where id=?\",\n                         (f\"2026-01-0{i + 1}T00:00:00+00:00\", pid))\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    home = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Story 6\" in home and \"Story 2\" in home\n    assert \"Story 1\" not in home and \"Story 0\" not in home\n    news = (out / \"news.html\").read_text()\n    assert news.index(\"Story 6\") < news.index(\"Story 3\") < news.index(\"Story 0\")\n\n\ndef test_drafts_are_absent(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, author, \"Secret Draft Title\", slug=\"secret-slug\", publish=False)\n    add(db_path, author, \"Public One\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    for text in site_text(out).values():\n        assert \"Secret Draft Title\" not in text and \"secret-slug\" not in text\n    assert not (out / \"posts\" / \"secret-slug.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_post_set_back_to_draft_is_absent_next_render(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n# Domain Docs\n\nHow the engineering skills should consume this repo's domain documentation when exploring the codebase.\n\n## Before exploring, read these\n\n- **`CONTEXT.md`** at the repo root: this repo's glossary.   out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    home = (out / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Story 6\" in home and \"Story 2\" in home\n    assert \"Story 1\" not in home and \"Story 0\" not in home\n    news = (out / \"news.html\").read_text()\n    assert news.index(\"Story 6\") < news.index(\"Story 3\") < news.index(\"Story 0\")\n\n\ndef test_drafts_are_absent(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, author, \"Secret Draft Title\", slug=\"secret-slug\", publish=False)\n    add(db_path, author, \"Public One\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    for text in site_text(out).values():\n        assert \"Secret Draft Title\" not in text and \"secret-slug\" not in text\n    assert not (out / \"posts\" / \"secret-slug.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_post_set_back_to_draft_is_absent_next_render(db_path, author, tmp_path):\n# Domain Docs\n\nHow the engineering skills should consume this repo's domain documentation when exploring the codebase.\n\n## Before exploring, read these\n\n- **`CONTEXT.md`** at the repo root: this repo's glossary. Some skills call this file `GLOSSARY.md`; here it is `CONTEXT.md`, so do not create a separate `GLOSSARY.md`.\n- **`docs/adr/`**: read ADRs that touch the area you're about to work in.\n\nIf any of these files don't exist, **proceed silently**. Don't flag their absence; don't suggest creating them upfront. The `/domain-modeling` skill (reached via `/grill-with-docs` and `/improve-codebase-architecture`) creates them lazily when terms or decisions actually get resolved.\n\n## File structure\n\n```\n/\n├── CONTEXT.md\n├── docs/adr/\n└── app/\n```\n\nThis repo is single-context.\n\n## Use the glossary's vocabulary\n\nWhen your output names a domain concept (in an issue title, a refactor proposal, a hypothesis, a test name), use the term as defined in `CONTEXT.md`. return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}{path_suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": token, **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n                           \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- create, slug, list ---------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_with_generated_slug(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Spring Rush: Kickoff!\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n        if password != getpass.getpass(\"Password again: \"):\n            print(\"The passwords did not match; nothing was created.\")\n            return 1\n        users.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        try:\n            users.create_user(settings.DATABASE_PATH, email=args.email,\n                              password=password, role=\"admin\",\n                              display_name=args.name)\n        except ValueError as exc:\n            print(f\"{exc} Nothing was created.\")\n            return 1\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n            print(f\"{args.email} already has an account.\")\n            return 1\n        print(f\"Created Admin {args.email}.\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        if settings.SECRET_KEY == settings.DEV_SECRET_KEY:\n            print(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY is not set (copy .env.example to .env and \"\n                  \"change it). Refusing to serve with the public dev key.\")\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        try:\n            out, items = render()\n        except DraftInOutput as exc:\n            print(exc)\n            return 1\n        print(\"Rendered:\")\n        for item in items:\n            print(f\"  - {item}\")\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        bad = deploy.root_absolute_files(settings.SITE)\n        if bad:\n            print(\"Refusing to deploy: root-absolute paths (href=\\\"/...\\\") in \"\n                  + \", \".join(str(b) for b in bad)\n                  + \". Run `uv run cms publish` again.\")\n            return 1\n        if confirm(\"Push site/ to gh-pages and make it Live? [y/N] \").strip().lower() not in (\"y\", \"yes\"):\n            print(\"Not deployed. Nothing was pushed.\")\n            return 0\n        code = push(settings.SITE, args.message)\n        if code != 0:\n            print(\"The push failed, so nothing was recorded as deployed.\")\n            return code\n        deploy.record_deploy(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n        print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. In GitHub: Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a \"\n              \"branch -> gh-pages / root. It can take up to 10 minutes to appear.\")\n        return 0\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\napp/posts.py:44:def init_posts(database: Path) -> monkeypatch.setattr(publish, \"_published_pages\",\n                        lambda db: publish._pages_where(db, \"1=1\"))\n    with pytest.raises(DraftInOutput, match=\"Leaky Page\"):\n import sqlite3\n    from app import settings\n    db = tmp_path / \"seed.db\"\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", \"scripts\" / \"seed_demo.py\")\n    seed = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(seed)\n    assert seed.main() == 0 and seed.main() == 0  # re-runnable\n    statuses = first, so each term's posts are too\n        for group, term in [(categories, e[\"category\"])] + [(tags, t) for t in e[\"tags\"]]:\n            if term:\n                group.setdefault(term[\"slug\"], {**term, \"items\": []})[\"items\"].append(e)\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > tests/test_taxonomy.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"F2: categories and tags on Posts, tested at the HTTP app and the Publish run.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import posts, users\nfrom app.cli import main\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save(c, post_id=None, **fields):\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    data = {\"title\": \"Rush Night\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": \"Body.\", \"action\": \"save\",\n            \"csrf_token\": token, **fields}\n    return c.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_id_of(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef add(db_path, title, category=\"\", tags=\"\", publish=True):\n    posts.init_posts(db_path)\n    return posts.create_post(db_path, author=users.get_user(db_path, 2), title=title,\n                             slug=\"\", body=\"Body.\", publish=publish,\n                             category=category, tags=tags)\n\n\ndef test_step1_category_and_tags_persist_after_save(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = post_id_of(save(c, category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush, social\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Events\"' in page\n    assert 'name=\"tags\" value=\"rush, social\"' in page\n    save(c, post_id=pid, category=\"Service\", tags=\"  Rush ,, rush, Fall  \")\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Service\"' in page\n    assert 'name=\"tags\" value=\"Rush, Fall\"' in page  # trimmed, blanks and repeats gone\n\n\ndef test_step1_post_form_refuses_a_post_without_csrf(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = post_id_of(save(c, category=\"Events\"))\n    response = c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\", data={\n        \"title\": \"Hijack\", \"category\": \"Evil\", \"action\": \"save\"},\n        follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (400, 403)\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Events\"' in c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n\n\ndef test_step2_content_list_filters_by_category(db_path, client_as):\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Events\")\n    add(db_path, \"Food Drive\", category=\"Service\")\n    add(db_path, \"No Category\")\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    everything = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in everything and \"Food Drive\" in everything\n    filtered = c.get(\"/admin/content?category=events\").text  # case-insensitive\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in filtered\n    assert \"Food Drive\" not in filtered and \"No Category\" not in filtered\n    assert '<option value=\"Service\"' in filtered  # the dropdown lists every category\n\n\ndef test_step3_publish_writes_category_and_tag_pages(db_path, tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):\n    from app import settings\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Chapter Events\", tags=\"rush, Big Social\")\n    add(db_path, \"Food Drive\", category=\"Service\", tags=\"rush\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db_path)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"SITE\", tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert main([\"publish\"]) == 0\n    site = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    category = (site / \"category\" / \"chapter-events.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in category and \"Food Drive\" not in category\n    rush = (site / \"tag\" / \"rush.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in rush and \"Food Drive\" in rush\n    assert rush.index(\"Food Drive\") < rush.index(\"Rush Night\")  # newest first\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in (site / \"tag\" / \"big-social.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"category/chapter-events.html\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n\n\ndef test_step4_post_links_to_its_terms_relatively(db_path, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Chapter Events\", tags=\"rush, Big Social\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    page = (out / \"posts\" / \"rush-night.html\").read_text()\n    for target in (\"category/chapter-events.html\", \"tag/rush.html\", \"tag/big-social.html\"):\n        assert f'href=\"../{target}\"' in page\n        assert (out / \"posts\" / f\"../{target}\").resolve().exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', f.read_text()), f\n    index = (out / \"tag\" / \"rush.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"../posts/rush-night.html\"' in index and 'href=\"../style.css\"' in index\n\n\ndef test_step5_draft_with_same_terms_is_absent_from_index_pages(db_path, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, \"Public Post\", category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush\")\n    add(db_path, \"Secret Draft\", category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush\", publish=False)\n    add(db_path, \"Lonely Draft\", category=\"Drafts Only\", tags=\"hidden-tag\", publish=False)\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    for index in (\"category/events.html\", \"tag/rush.html\"):\n        text = (out / index).read_text()\n        assert \"Public Post\" in text\n        assert \"Secret Draft\" not in text and \"secret-draft\" not in text\n    assert not (out / \"category\" / \"drafts-only.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"tag\" / \"hidden-tag.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Drafts Only\" not in \"\".join(f.read_text() for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q 2>&1 | tail I'll simplify it, then write the tests."},{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018cZoB2PzPUSRevFpvrZP9Z","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='app/publish.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold=s[s.index(\"    for e in entries:  # entries are newest\"):s.index(\"    page_entries = []\")]\nnew='''    for e in entries:  # entries are newest first, so each term's posts are too\n        for group, term in [(categories, e[\"category\"])] + [(tags, t) for t in e[\"tags\"]]:\n            if term:\n                group.setdefault(term[\"slug\"], {**term, \"items\": []})[\"items\"].append(e)\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old,new)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > tests/test_taxonomy.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"F2: categories and tags on Posts, tested at the HTTP app and the Publish run.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import posts, users\nfrom app.cli import main\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save(c, post_id=None, **fields):\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    data = {\"title\": \"Rush Night\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": \"Body.\", \"action\": \"save\",\n            \"csrf_token\": token, **fields}\n    return c.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_id_of(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef add(db_path, title, category=\"\", tags=\"\", publish=True):\n    posts.init_posts(db_path)\n    return posts.create_post(db_path, author=users.get_user(db_path, 2), title=title,\n                             slug=\"\", body=\"Body.\", publish=publish,\n                             category=category, tags=tags)\n\n\ndef test_step1_category_and_tags_persist_after_save(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = post_id_of(save(c, category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush, social\"))\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Events\"' in page\n    assert 'name=\"tags\" value=\"rush, social\"' in page\n    save(c, post_id=pid, category=\"Service\", tags=\"  Rush ,, rush, Fall  \")\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Service\"' in page\n    assert 'name=\"tags\" value=\"Rush, Fall\"' in page  # trimmed, blanks and repeats gone\n\n\ndef test_step1_post_form_refuses_a_post_without_csrf(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = post_id_of(save(c, category=\"Events\"))\n    response = c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\", data={\n        \"title\": \"Hijack\", \"category\": \"Evil\", \"action\": \"save\"},\n        follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (400, 403)\n    assert 'name=\"category\" value=\"Events\"' in c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{pid}\").text\n\n\ndef test_step2_content_list_filters_by_category(db_path, client_as):\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Events\")\n    add(db_path, \"Food Drive\", category=\"Service\")\n    add(db_path, \"No Category\")\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    everything = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in everything and \"Food Drive\" in everything\n    filtered = c.get(\"/admin/content?category=events\").text  # case-insensitive\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in filtered\n    assert \"Food Drive\" not in filtered and \"No Category\" not in filtered\n    assert '<option value=\"Service\"' in filtered  # the dropdown lists every category\n\n\ndef test_step3_publish_writes_category_and_tag_pages(db_path, tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):\n    from app import settings\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Chapter Events\", tags=\"rush, Big Social\")\n    add(db_path, \"Food Drive\", category=\"Service\", tags=\"rush\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db_path)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"SITE\", tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert main([\"publish\"]) == 0\n    site = tmp_path / \"site\"\n    category = (site / \"category\" / \"chapter-events.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in category and \"Food Drive\" not in category\n    rush = (site / \"tag\" / \"rush.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in rush and \"Food Drive\" in rush\n    assert rush.index(\"Food Drive\") < rush.index(\"Rush Night\")  # newest first\n    assert \"Rush Night\" in (site / \"tag\" / \"big-social.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"category/chapter-events.html\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n\n\ndef test_step4_post_links_to_its_terms_relatively(db_path, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, \"Rush Night\", category=\"Chapter Events\", tags=\"rush, Big Social\")\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    page = (out / \"posts\" / \"rush-night.html\").read_text()\n    for target in (\"category/chapter-events.html\", \"tag/rush.html\", \"tag/big-social.html\"):\n        assert f'href=\"../{target}\"' in page\n        assert (out / \"posts\" / f\"../{target}\").resolve().exists()\n    for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', f.read_text()), f\n    index = (out / \"tag\" / \"rush.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"../posts/rush-night.html\"' in index and 'href=\"../style.css\"' in index\n\n\ndef test_step5_draft_with_same_terms_is_absent_from_index_pages(db_path, tmp_path):\n    add(db_path, \"Public Post\", category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush\")\n    add(db_path, \"Secret Draft\", category=\"Events\", tags=\"rush\", publish=False)\n    add(db_path, \"Lonely Draft\", category=\"Drafts Only\", tags=\"hidden-tag\", publish=False)\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database=db_path)\n    for index in (\"category/events.html\", \"tag/rush.html\"):\n        text = (out / index).read_text()\n        assert \"Public Post\" in text\n        assert \"Secret Draft\" not in text and \"secret-draft\" not in text\n    assert not (out / \"category\" / \"drafts-only.html\").exists()\n    assert not (out / \"tag\" / \"hidden-tag.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Drafts Only\" not in \"\".join(f.read_text() for f in out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\nEOF\nuv run pytest -q 2>&1 | tail -15"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}]}}

{"parentUuid":"b6b76a4e-da56-405f-ae2a-191980c0a499","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CgESnUAC2814LxcvLqxo2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Categories and tags are in. Against a fresh seeded database the full suite passes (200 tests). I haven't committed anything.\n\n**What it does**\n- **Post form:** it has a single Category field and one comma-separated Tags field. Both save on create and edit. Tags are trimmed, and blanks and repeats are dropped.\n- **Content list:** it has a Category dropdown and filters by category only. The match ignores case, and filtering by category shows Posts only, since Pages have no category.\n- **`cms publish`:** it writes `site/category/<slug>.html` and `site/tag/<slug>.html`, each listing only published posts, newest first. A category or tag used only by drafts gets no page. Each post page links to its category and tags with `../` paths, and the publish summary lists the new pages.\n- **Rules:** I added no new forms, settings or dependencies. The existing post form's CSRF check still refuses a POST without a token, and `test_step1_post_form_refuses_a_post_without_csrf` covers that. New templates use relative links or `url_for`. Because there are no new settings, `.env.example` is unchanged.\n- **Tests and docs:** the new tests are in `tests/test_taxonomy.py`, named `test_step1_…` through `test_step5_…`. Category and Tag are added to `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n**Run this:** `rm cms.db && uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py`. The `posts` table gained `category` and `tags` columns and I wrote no migration. The seed script now adds a category and tags to its demo posts. It ran clean twice from the `.env.example` values. Until you recreate `cms.db`, `tests/test_t00.py::test_publish_writes_a_site` and `::test_published_html_uses_relative_paths` fail with an `IndexError`, because they publish from that old database.\n\n**Existing test change, as you directed:** `test_list_has_no_categories_tags_or_bulk_actions` in `tests/test_dashboard.py` dropped only `\"categor\"`. The `\"tags\"`, `\"bulk\"` and checkbox assertions and the test name are unchanged.\n\n**Left for you**\n- The `/code-review` findings comment on #16 isn't posted yet.
